# Domain Subsystem Matrix

**Document ID:** ATC-DOMAIN-SUBSYSTEM-MATRIX-REGISTRY-001  
**Status:** ARCHITECTURE CONTRACT  
**Domains:** 411  
**Subsystem matrices:** 411  
**Subsystems per domain:** 8

Each canonical domain receives its own subsystem matrix. The matrix is decomposed into Core/Lifecycle, Contract/Schema, Source/Implementation, State/Data, Event/Flow, Integration/Interface, Security/Authority and Testing/Evidence/Verification.
